<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A multipack of $12$ cans of Ned's Cola costs $\pounds 6.95$. 

A single can of Ned's Cola costs $65 \ \text{pence}$ if bought individually. 

Calculate how much cheaper it is to buy a multipack of $12$ cans than to buy $12$ cans individually, giving your answer in pence.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A multipack of &#36;12&#36; cans of Ned's Cola costs &#36;\pounds 6.95&#36;. 

A single can of Ned's Cola costs &#36;65 \ \text{pence}&#36; if bought individually. 

Calculate how much cheaper it is to buy a multipack of &#36;12&#36; cans than to buy &#36;12&#36; cans individually, giving your answer in pence.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of 12 cans}                 &= 12 \times 65 \\\\
                                        &= 780 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text {Cheaper by}                      &= 780 \ \text{p} - \pounds 6.95 \\\\
                                        &= 780 \ \text{p} - 695 \ \text{p} \\\\
                                        &= 85 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$85 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A multipack of $15$ packets of crisps costs $\pounds 3.90$.

A packet of crisps costs $35 \ \text{pence}$ if they are bought individually.

Calculate how much cheaper it is to buy a multipack of $15$ packets of crisps than to buy $15$ packets individually.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A multipack of &#36;15&#36; packets of crisps costs &#36;\pounds 3.90&#36;.

A packet of crisps costs &#36;35 \ \text{pence}&#36; if they are bought individually.

Calculate how much cheaper it is to buy a multipack of &#36;15&#36; packets of crisps than to buy &#36;15&#36; packets individually.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of 15 packets}  &= 15 \times 35 \\\\
                            &= 525 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text {Cheaper by}          &= 525 \ \text{p} - \pounds 3.90 \\\\
                            &= 525 \ \text{p} - 390 \ \text{p} \\\\
                            &= 135 \ \text{p} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 1.35
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 1.35$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Clare went to the sweet shop with her weekly pocket money. She saw that lollipops cost $35 \ \text{p}$, 
and she worked out that if she bought $8$ lollipops she would end up with $30 \ \text{p}$ left from her pocket money.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>

and she worked out that if she bought &#36;8&#36; lollipops she would end up with &#36;30 \ \text{p}&#36; left from her pocket money.
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="answers">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

She then worked out that if she bought only $5$ lollipops, she would have exactly the right amount of money left over to buy $3$ packets of jelly gums.

Work out the price of one packet of jelly gum.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">She then worked out that if she bought only &#36;5&#36; lollipops, she would have exactly the right amount of money left over to buy &#36;3&#36; packets of jelly gums.

Work out the price of one packet of jelly gum.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of 5 lollipops}     &= 5 \times 35 \\\\
                                &= 175 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text {Cost of 3 jelly gums}    &= 310 - 175 \\\\
\text {Cost of 1 jelly gums}    &= \dfrac {1} {3} \times (310 - 175) \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {135} {3} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {45 \times 3} {3} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {45 \times \cancel{3}} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
                                &= 45 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$45 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Jon has $30\%$ more money than Miguel and together they have $\pounds 46$. Calculate how much money Jon has. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Jon has &#36;30\%&#36; more money than Miguel and together they have &#36;\pounds 46&#36;. Calculate how much money Jon has. 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Miguel has (in $\pounds$) $= a$

Jon has (in $\pounds$) $= a + 0.3 \times a = 1.3a$

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
a + 1.3a                        &= 46 \\\\
2.3a                            &= 46 \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {46} {2.3} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {46 \times 10} {23} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {23 \times 2 \times 10} {23} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {\cancel{23} \times 2 \times 10} {\cancel{23}} \\\\
a                               &= 2 \times 10 \\\\
a                               &= \pounds 20 \\\\
\text {Jon has}                 &= 1.3 \times a \\\\
                                &= 1.3 \times 20 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 26
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 26$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let Miguel has (in &#36;\pounds&#36;) &#36;= a&#36;

Jon has (in &#36;\pounds&#36;) &#36;= a + 0.3 \times a = 1.3a&#36;

rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_question

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
a + 1.3a                        &= 46 \\\\
2.3a                            &= 46 \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {46} {2.3} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {46 \times 10} {23} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {23 \times 2 \times 10} {23} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {\cancel{23} \times 2 \times 10} {\cancel{23}} \\\\
a                               &= 2 \times 10 \\\\
a                               &= \pounds 20 \\\\
\text {Jon has}                 &= 1.3 \times a \\\\
                                &= 1.3 \times 20 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 26
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

**Question**

One third of $\pounds 120$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">One third of &#36;\pounds 120&#36;.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {3} \ \text {of} \ \pounds 120 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {3} \times 120 \\\\
&= \dfrac {120} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 40} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 40} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
&= \pounds 40
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A pack of $15$ pens cost $\pounds 4.65$.
Pens are the same value if they are bought individually.

What is the correct calculation to work out the cost of $19$ pens?

-  $(4.65 \times 15) \div 19$
-  $(15 \div 4.65) \times 19$
-  $(4.65 \div 15) \times 19$
-  $(15 \div 4.65) \div 19$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A pack of &#36;15&#36; pens cost &#36;\pounds 4.65&#36;.
Pens are the same value if they are bought individually.

What is the correct calculation to work out the cost of &#36;19&#36; pens?

-  &#36;(4.65 \times 15) \div 19&#36;
-  &#36;(15 \div 4.65) \times 19&#36;
-  &#36;(4.65 \div 15) \times 19&#36;
-  &#36;(15 \div 4.65) \div 19&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="uuid">

<div class="papername">

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
tmoney
</span>
</li>

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Maggie buys

$1 \dfrac{1}{2} \ \text{kg}$ of carrots at $64 \ \text{p}$ per kg,

$4$ cakes at $35 \ \text{p}$ each,

$6$ tins of soup at $43 \ \text{p}$ each.

She pays with a $\pounds 10$ note.

Work out how much change she should get. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Maggie buys

&#36;1 \dfrac{1}{2} \ \text{kg}&#36; of carrots at &#36;64 \ \text{p}&#36; per kg,

&#36;4&#36; cakes at &#36;35 \ \text{p}&#36; each,

&#36;6&#36; tins of soup at &#36;43 \ \text{p}&#36; each.

She pays with a &#36;\pounds 10&#36; note.

Work out how much change she should get. 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 1 \dfrac {1} {2} \times 64 + 4 \times 35 + 6 \times 43 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 64} {2} + 4 \times 35 + 6 \times 43 \\\\
                        &= 3 \times 32 + 4 \times 35 + 6 \times 43 \\\\
                        &= 96 + 140 + 258 \\\\
                        &= 494 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 4.94 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 10 - 4.94 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5.06
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 5.06$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

</div>

</code></pre>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 64} {2} + 4 \times 35 + 6 \times 43 \\\\
                        &= 3 \times 32 + 4 \times 35 + 6 \times 43 \\\\
                        &= 96 + 140 + 258 \\\\
                        &= 494 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 4.94 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 10 - 4.94 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5.06
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #15803d; border-radius: 999px; background: #dcfce7; color: #14532d; padding: 0.24rem 0.7rem; font-size: 0.95rem; font-weight: 700;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Laura's family wins $\pounds 54$ in a raffle. They give $\pounds 12$ to charity and share the rest equally between the 
five family members. How much does each family member get?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Laura's family wins &#36;\pounds 54&#36; in a raffle. They give &#36;\pounds 12&#36; to charity and share the rest equally between the 
five family members. How much does each family member get?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Family member share}     &= \dfrac {54 - 12} {5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {42} {5} \\\\
                                &= \pounds 8.40
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 8.40$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The charity spends $60\%$ of the donation on buying equipment. How much of the donation is not spent?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The charity spends &#36;60\%&#36; of the donation on buying equipment. How much of the donation is not spent?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the charity spends $60 \%$ on equipments, then the donation not spent is $100 - 60 = 40 \%$.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Donation not spent}   &= 40 \% \ \text {of} \ \pounds 12 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {40} {100} \times 12 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {40 \times 12} {100} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {480} {100} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 4.80
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 4.80$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculators normally cost $\pounds 6$ and protractors normally cost $30 \ \text{p}$.

In a sale, calculators have been reduced by a quarter and protractors are half price.

David bought $8$ calculators and $20$ protractors.

How much did he have to pay? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculators normally cost &#36;\pounds 6&#36; and protractors normally cost &#36;30 \ \text{p}&#36;.

In a sale, calculators have been reduced by a quarter and protractors are half price.

David bought &#36;8&#36; calculators and &#36;20&#36; protractors.

How much did he have to pay? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the calculator cost is reduced by one quarter, then the new cost will be $1 - \dfrac {1} {4} = \dfrac {3} {4}$ of the original cost.

If the protractor cost is reduced by half, then the new cost will be $1 - \dfrac {1} {2} = \dfrac {1} {2}$ of the original cost.

Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total cost}   &= 8 \times \dfrac {3} {4} \times \pounds 6 + 20 \times \dfrac {1} {2} \times 30 \ \text{p} \\\\
                    &= 8 \times \dfrac{3}{4} \times \pounds 6 + 20 \times \dfrac {1} {2} \times \pounds 0.30 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{8 \times 3 \times 6}{4} + \dfrac {20 \times 0.3}{2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{4 \times 2 \times 3 \times 6}{4} + \dfrac {10 \times 2 \times 0.3}{2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{\cancel{4} \times 2 \times 3 \times 6}{\cancel{4}} + \dfrac {10 \times \cancel{2} \times 0.3}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
                    &= 2 \times 3 \times 6 + 10 \times 0.3 \\\\
                    &= 36 + 3 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 39
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 39$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_unit_no_dot
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If the calculator cost is reduced by one quarter, then the new cost will be &#36;1 - \dfrac {1} {4} = \dfrac {3} {4}&#36; of the original cost.

If the protractor cost is reduced by half, then the new cost will be &#36;1 - \dfrac {1} {2} = \dfrac {1} {2}&#36; of the original cost.

Using that, we get

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total cost}   &= 8 \times \dfrac {3} {4} \times \pounds 6 + 20 \times \dfrac {1} {2} \times 30 \ \text{p} \\\\
                    &= 8 \times \dfrac{3}{4} \times \pounds 6 + 20 \times \dfrac {1} {2} \times \pounds 0.30 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{8 \times 3 \times 6}{4} + \dfrac {20 \times 0.3}{2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{4 \times 2 \times 3 \times 6}{4} + \dfrac {10 \times 2 \times 0.3}{2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{\cancel{4} \times 2 \times 3 \times 6}{\cancel{4}} + \dfrac {10 \times \cancel{2} \times 0.3}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
                    &= 2 \times 3 \times 6 + 10 \times 0.3 \\\\
                    &= 36 + 3 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 39
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

*SPECIAL OFFER ON THE MATHS BOOKS!*

*Fun Trignometric Calculus For Pre-School, by Ian Tregation*

*Only $\pounds16$ each.*

*Special offer: Buy $3$ and get $1$ free!*

Subhan wants to get $5$ of these books.

How much does he pay?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">*SPECIAL OFFER ON THE MATHS BOOKS!*

*Fun Trignometric Calculus For Pre-School, by Ian Tregation*

*Only &#36;\pounds16&#36; each.*

*Special offer: Buy &#36;3&#36; and get &#36;1&#36; free!*

Subhan wants to get &#36;5&#36; of these books.

How much does he pay?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If Subhan buys $3$ book, he gets the $4 ^ \text{th}$ book free. And then he pays for $5 ^ \text{th}$ book.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= 3 \times 16 + 16 \\\\
                    &= 48 + 16 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 64
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Anokhi buys $4$ chocolate bars at $75 \ \text{p}$ each and $3$ pens at $95 \ \text{p}$ each.
How much change does she receive from a $\pounds10$ note?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Anokhi buys &#36;4&#36; chocolate bars at &#36;75 \ \text{p}&#36; each and &#36;3&#36; pens at &#36;95 \ \text{p}&#36; each.
How much change does she receive from a &#36;\pounds10&#36; note?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 4 \times 75 + 3 \times 95 \\\\
                        &= 300 + 285 \\\\
                        &= 585 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5.85 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 10 - 5.85 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 4.15
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 4.15$

</div>
</div>

                        &= 300 + 285 \\\\
                        &= 585 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5.85 \\\\

                        &= \pounds 4.15

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Gurveen is buying paint and there are three sizes of can to choose from:

One $2 \ \text{litre}$ can cost $\pounds 15$

One $3 \ \text{litre}$ can cost $\pounds20$

One $4 \ \text{litre}$ can cost $\pounds25$

Which size is the best value for money?

Justify your answer carefully.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Gurveen is buying paint and there are three sizes of can to choose from:

One &#36;2 \ \text{litre}&#36; can cost &#36;\pounds 15&#36;

One &#36;3 \ \text{litre}&#36; can cost &#36;\pounds20&#36;

One &#36;4 \ \text{litre}&#36; can cost &#36;\pounds25&#36;

Which size is the best value for money?

Justify your answer carefully.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let's calculate the cost per litre for the three sizes.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {First can}   &= \dfrac {15} {2} \\\\
                    &= \pounds 7.50 \\\\
\text {Second can}  &= \dfrac {20} {3} \\\\
                    &= \pounds 6.33 \\\\
\text {Third can}   &= \dfrac {25} {4} \\\\
                    &= \pounds 6.25
\end{aligned}
$

Best value $= 4 \ \text {litre}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4 \ \text {litre}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let's calculate the cost per litre for the three sizes.

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text {First can}   &= \dfrac {15} {2} \\\\
                    &= \pounds 7.50 \\\\
\text {Second can}  &= \dfrac {20} {3} \\\\
                    &= \pounds 6.33 \\\\
\text {Third can}   &= \dfrac {25} {4} \\\\
                    &= \pounds 6.25
\end{aligned}
&#36;

Best value &#36;= 4 \ \text {litre}&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;4 \ \text {litre}&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>


<div class="review">
<ul class="review">
<li class="review">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Janice wants to buy as many cookies as she can afford with a $\pounds 10$ note. Each cookie costs $70 \ \text{p}$. 
How much change will she receive? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Janice wants to buy as many cookies as she can afford with a &#36;\pounds 10&#36; note. Each cookie costs &#36;70 \ \text{p}&#36;. 
How much change will she receive? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cookies she can buy}   &= \dfrac {\pounds 10} {70 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {1000 \ \text{p}} {70 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                                    &= 14 \text{ remainder } 20 \\\\
                                    &\approx 14 \\\\
\text {Change received}             &= 20 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cookies she can buy}   &= \dfrac {\pounds 10} {70 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {1000 \ \text{p}} {70 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                                    &= 14 \text{ remainder } 20 \\\\
                                    &\approx 14 \\\\
\text {Change received}             &= 20 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;20 \ \text{p}&#36;

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Karim buys $7$ burgers and $5$ steaks. Burgers cost $61 \ \text{p}$ each and steaks cost $84 \ \text{p}$ each.

How much Karim spend in total?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Karim buys &#36;7&#36; burgers and &#36;5&#36; steaks. Burgers cost &#36;61 \ \text{p}&#36; each and steaks cost &#36;84 \ \text{p}&#36; each.

How much Karim spend in total?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 7 \times 61  + 5 \times 84  \\\\
                        &= 427 + 420 \\\\
                        &= 847 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 8.47
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 8.47$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Sausages come in packs of $8$. Each pack costs $\pounds 1.30$.

James spends $\pounds 9.10$ on sausages. How much sausages did James get?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Sausages come in packs of &#36;8&#36;. Each pack costs &#36;\pounds 1.30&#36;.

James spends &#36;\pounds 9.10&#36; on sausages. How much sausages did James get?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">

</div>

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total packs}                 &= \dfrac {9.10} {1.30}  \\\\
\text {Total sausages}              &= \dfrac {9.10} {1.30} \times 8 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {9.10 \times 100 \times 8} {1.30 \times 100}  \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {910 \times 8} {130} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {7 \times 130 \times 8} {130} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {7 \times \cancel {130} \times 8} {\cancel {130}} \\\\
                                    &= 7 \times 8 \\\\
                                    &= 56
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Tyler buys $16$ chicken wings, and receives $\pounds 14.72$ change from a $\pounds 20$ note.

What is the price of a chicken wing?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Tyler buys &#36;16&#36; chicken wings, and receives &#36;\pounds 14.72&#36; change from a &#36;\pounds 20&#36; note.

What is the price of a chicken wing?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}                   &= 20 - 14.72 \\\\
                                      &= \pounds 5.28 \\\\
                                      &= 528 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text {Cost of a chicken wing}        &= \dfrac {528} {16} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {16 \times 33} {16} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {\cancel {16} \times 33} {\cancel {16}} \\\\
                                      &= 33 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The butcher also sells slices of turkey.

Donald buys three quarters of the slices in the shop. Alisha then buys a sixth of the remaining slices.

Alisha bought $3$ slices. How many more slices than Alisha did Donald buy?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The butcher also sells slices of turkey.

Donald buys three quarters of the slices in the shop. Alisha then buys a sixth of the remaining slices.

Alisha bought &#36;3&#36; slices. How many more slices than Alisha did Donald buy?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let total number of slices $= a$

Number of slices Donald bought $= \dfrac {3a} {4}$

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {1} {6} \times (a - \dfrac {3a} {4}) &= 3\\\\
a - \dfrac {3a} {4}                        &= 3 \times 6\\\\
\dfrac {a \times 4} {4} - \dfrac {3a} {4}   &= 3 \times 6 \\\\
\dfrac {4a} {4} - \dfrac {3a} {4}           &= 3 \times 6 \\\\
\dfrac {4a - 3a} {4}                        &= 3 \times 6 \\\\
\dfrac {a} {4}                            &= 3 \times 6 \\\\
a                                          &= 3 \times 6 \times 4 \\\\
a                                          &= 72 \\\\
\text {Slices Donald bought}               &= \dfrac {3a} {4} \\\\
                                           &= \dfrac {3 \times 72} {4} \\\\
                                           &= 54 \\\\
\text {Difference}                         &= 54 - 3 \\\\
                                           &= 51
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$51$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Lamb chops are three times more expensive than pork chops.

Tina buys $2$ lamb chops and $2$ pork chops. She spends $\pounds 9.60$ in total.

How much do lamb chops and pork chops cost?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Lamb chops are three times more expensive than pork chops.

Tina buys &#36;2&#36; lamb chops and &#36;2&#36; pork chops. She spends &#36;\pounds 9.60&#36; in total.

How much do lamb chops and pork chops cost?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the cost of a pork chop $= a$

Cost of a lamb chop $= 3a$

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
2 \times 3a + 2 \times a    &= 9.60 \\\\
6a + 2a                     &= 9.60 \\\\
8a                          &= 9.60 \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {9.60} {8} \\\\
a                           &= \pounds 1.20
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of a pork chop}  &= \pounds 1.20 \\\\
\text {Cost of a lamb chop} &= 3a \\\\
                            &= 3 \times 1.20 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 3.60
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Pork chop $=$ $\pounds 1.20$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Lamb chop $=$ $\pounds 3.60$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Delon buys $17$ carrots and $6$ turnips.
Carrots cost $8\ \text{p}$ each and turnips cost $13\ \text{p}$ each.

How much did Delon spend in total?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Delon buys &#36;17&#36; carrots and &#36;6&#36; turnips.
Carrots cost &#36;8\ \text{p}&#36; each and turnips cost &#36;13\ \text{p}&#36; each.

How much did Delon spend in total?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent} &= 17 \times 8 + 6 \times 13 \\\\
                    &= 136 + 78 \\\\
                    &= 214 \ \text{p} \\\\
                    &= \pounds 2.14
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 2.14$

</div>
</div>

</span>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Potatoes are only sold in sacks of $12$ potatoes. One of these sacks costs $56 \ \text{p}$.
Stanley has $\pounds 3.20$.

How many potatoes can Stanley buy, and how much money does he have left over?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Potatoes are only sold in sacks of &#36;12&#36; potatoes. One of these sacks costs &#36;56 \ \text{p}&#36;.
Stanley has &#36;\pounds 3.20&#36;.

How many potatoes can Stanley buy, and how much money does he have left over?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total sacks}                 &= \dfrac {\pounds 3.20} {56 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {320 \ \text{p}} {56 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {8 \times 40} {8 \times 7} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {8} \times 40} {\cancel {8} \times 7} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {40} {7} \\\\
                                    &= 5 \ \text {remainder} \ 5 \\\\
                                    &\approx 5 \\\\
\text {Total potatoes}              &= 12 \times 5 \\\\
                                    &= 60 \\\\
\text {Money left}                  &= \pounds 3.20 - 5 \times 56 \ \text{p} \\\\
                                    &= 320 \ \text{p} - 280 \ \text{p} \\\\
                                    &= 40 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

Potatoes $=$ $60$

**Question**

What is the price of a pepper?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>

\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$5$ giant mushrooms cost the same as $3$ cucumbers.
Marvin buys $12$ cucumbers for $\pounds 3.60$.

What is the cost of $8$ giant mushrooms?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;5&#36; giant mushrooms cost the same as &#36;3&#36; cucumbers.
Marvin buys &#36;12&#36; cucumbers for &#36;\pounds 3.60&#36;.

What is the cost of &#36;8&#36; giant mushrooms?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of cucumber}         &= \dfrac {\pounds 3.60} {12} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3.60 \times 100 \ \text{p}} {12} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {360} {12} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {12 \times 30} {12} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {12} \times 30} {\cancel {12}} \\\\
                                &= 30 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$

if $5$ mushrooms cost the same as $3$ cucumbers, then the cost of $1$ mushroom is $\dfrac {3} {5}$ of the cost of a cucumber.

Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of 1 mushroom}      &= \dfrac {3} {5} \times 30 \\\\
\text {Cost of 8 mushrooms}     &= 8 \times \dfrac {3} {5} \times 30 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {8 \times 3 \times 30} {5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {8 \times 3 \times 6 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {8 \times 3 \times 6 \times \cancel{5}} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
                                &= 144 \ \text{p} \\\\
                                &= \pounds 1.44
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 1.44$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

                                &= \dfrac {3.60 \times 100 \ \text{p}} {12} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {360} {12} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {12 \times 30} {12} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {12} \times 30} {\cancel {12}} \\\\
                                &= 30 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
&#36;

if &#36;5&#36; mushrooms cost the same as &#36;3&#36; cucumbers, then the cost of &#36;1&#36; mushroom is &#36;\dfrac {3} {5}&#36; of the cost of a cucumber.

Using that, we get

                                &= \dfrac {8 \times 3 \times 30} {5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {8 \times 3 \times 6 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {8 \times 3 \times 6 \times \cancel{5}} {\cancel{5}} \\\\

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Asha sells vegetables at the market. On Monday she sold half of her vegetables. On Tuesday she sold a third of the vegetables she had 
left.

What fraction of her original stock does she have left to sell on Wednesday?


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Asha sells vegetables at the market. On Monday she sold half of her vegetables. On Tuesday she sold a third of the vegetables she had 
left.

What fraction of her original stock does she have left to sell on Wednesday?

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the original stock of vegetables $= a$

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Vegetables sold on Monday}          &= \dfrac {a} {2} \\\\
\text {Vegetables sold on Tuesday}         &= \dfrac {1} {3} \times (a - \dfrac {a} {2}) \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {1} {3} \times \dfrac {a} {2} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {a} {6} \\\\
\text {Vegetables left to sell on Wednesday}    &= a - \dfrac {a} {2} - \dfrac {a} {6} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {a \times 6} {6} - \dfrac {a \times 3} {2 \times 3} - \dfrac {a} {6} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {6a - 3a - a} {6} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {2a} {6} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {a} {3} \\\\
\text {Fraction of vegetables left}             &= \dfrac {\text{Vegetables left}} {\text{Total vegetables}} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {a} {3} \div a \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {a} {3 \times a} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{a}} {3 \times \cancel{a}} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {1} {3}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {1} {3}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the original stock of vegetables &#36;= a&#36;

rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_question

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text {Vegetables sold on Monday}          &= \dfrac {a} {2} \\\\
\text {Vegetables sold on Tuesday}         &= \dfrac {1} {3} \times (a - \dfrac {a} {2}) \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {1} {3} \times \dfrac {a} {2} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {a} {6} \\\\
\text {Vegetables left to sell on Wednesday}    &= a - \dfrac {a} {2} - \dfrac {a} {6} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {a \times 6} {6} - \dfrac {a \times 3} {2 \times 3} - \dfrac {a} {6} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {6a - 3a - a} {6} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {2a} {6} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {a} {3} \\\\
\text {Fraction of vegetables left}             &= \dfrac {\text{Vegetables left}} {\text{Total vegetables}} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {a} {3} \div a \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {a} {3 \times a} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{a}} {3 \times \cancel{a}} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {1} {3}
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A tin of cookies costs $\pounds 3.69$ and a bag of jelly babies cost $\pounds 1.55$. 
Tracey and Cat buy $2$ tins of cookies and a bag of jelly babies. They pay with a $\pounds 20$ note. 
How much change should they get? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A tin of cookies costs &#36;\pounds 3.69&#36; and a bag of jelly babies cost &#36;\pounds 1.55&#36;. 
Tracey and Cat buy &#36;2&#36; tins of cookies and a bag of jelly babies. They pay with a &#36;\pounds 20&#36; note. 
How much change should they get? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}      &= 2 \times 3.69 + 1.55 \\\\
                         &= 7.38 + 1.55 \\\\
                         &= \pounds 8.93 \\\\
\text {Change received}  &= 20 - 8.93 \\\\
                         &= \pounds 11.07
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 11.07$

</div>
</div>

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #15803d; border-radius: 999px; background: #dcfce7; color: #14532d; padding: 0.24rem 0.7rem; font-size: 0.95rem; font-weight: 700;">
inherits parent tags: yes
</span>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A normal tin contains $40$ cookies. A special offer tin contains $\dfrac{3}{10}$ more cookies. 

Work out how many cookies there are in a special offer tin.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A normal tin contains &#36;40&#36; cookies. A special offer tin contains &#36;\dfrac{3}{10}&#36; more cookies. 

Work out how many cookies there are in a special offer tin.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total Cookies}   &= 40 + \dfrac {3} {10} \ \text {of} \ 40 \\\\
                        &= 40 + \dfrac {3} {10} \times 40 \\\\
                        &= 40 + \dfrac {3 \times 40} {10} \\\\
                        &= 40 + \dfrac {3 \times 4 \times 10} {10} \\\\
                        &= 40 + \dfrac {3 \times 4 \times \cancel {10}} {\cancel {10}} \\\\
                        &= 40 + 3 \times 4 \\\\
                        &= 40 + 12 \\\\
                        &= 52
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$52$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                        &= 40 + \dfrac {3} {10} \times 40 \\\\
                        &= 40 + \dfrac {3 \times 40} {10} \\\\
                        &= 40 + \dfrac {3 \times 4 \times 10} {10} \\\\
                        &= 40 + \dfrac {3 \times 4 \times \cancel {10}} {\cancel {10}} \\\\

                        &= 40 + 12 \\\\

\end{aligned}

</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Natalie buys $3$ small bags of peanuts and pays with a $\pounds 2$ coin. 
She receives $80 \ \text{p}$ change. 
How much does each bag cost? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Natalie buys &#36;3&#36; small bags of peanuts and pays with a &#36;\pounds 2&#36; coin. 
She receives &#36;80 \ \text{p}&#36; change. 
How much does each bag cost? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the cost of a bag (in pence) $= a$

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
3a + 80 \ \text{p}   &= \pounds 2 \\\\
3a + 80 \ \text{p}   &= 200 \ \text{p} \\\\
3a                  &= 200 - 80 \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {120} {3} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {3 \times 40} {3} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 40} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
a                   &= 40 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$

Cost of a bag $= 40 \ \text{p}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$40 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the cost of a bag (in pence) &#36;= a&#36;

rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_question

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
3a + 80 \ \text{p}   &= \pounds 2 \\\\

a                   &= \dfrac {120} {3} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {3 \times 40} {3} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 40} {\cancel {3}} \\\\

\end{aligned}
&#36;

Cost of a bag &#36;= 40 \ \text{p}&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many $26 \ \text{p}$ stamps can Antonia buy for $\pounds 10$ and how much change will she receive? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many &#36;26 \ \text{p}&#36; stamps can Antonia buy for &#36;\pounds 10&#36; and how much change will she receive? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total stamps}    &= \dfrac {\pounds 10} {26 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1000 \ \text{p}} {26 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1000} {26} \\\\
                        &= 38 \ \text {remainder} \ 12 \\\\
                        &\approx 38 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 1000 - 38 \times 26 \\\\
                        &= 1000 - 988 \\\\
                        &= 12 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$38$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$12 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

                        &= \dfrac {1000 \ \text{p}} {26 \ \text{p}} \\\\

                        &= 38 \ \text {remainder} \ 12 \\\\
                        &\approx 38 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 1000 - 38 \times 26 \\\\

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

To encourage her to tidy her bedroom, Cathy is given $1 \ \text{p}$ on the first day, $2 \ \text{p}$ on the second day, $4 \ \text{p}$ on the third day and so on, doubling the amount each day. 
How much has she been given after $8$ days?  

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">To encourage her to tidy her bedroom, Cathy is given &#36;1 \ \text{p}&#36; on the first day, &#36;2 \ \text{p}&#36; on the second day, &#36;4 \ \text{p}&#36; on the third day and so on, doubling the amount each day. 
How much has she been given after &#36;8&#36; days?  
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total money given}   &= 1 + 2 + 4 + 8 + 16 + 32 + 64 + 128 \\\\
                            &= 255 \ \text{p} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 2.55
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 2.55$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Gavin buys four bottles of cola at $\pounds 1.09$ each and $8$ chocolate bars at $62 \ \text{p}$. How much change should he receive from a ten-pound note? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Gavin buys four bottles of cola at &#36;\pounds 1.09&#36; each and &#36;8&#36; chocolate bars at &#36;62 \ \text{p}&#36;. How much change should he receive from a ten-pound note? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 4 \times \pounds 1.09 + 62 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 4.36 + \pounds 0.62 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 4.98 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 10 - 4.98 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5.02
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 5.02$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Mary goes to the school fair.

She pays to get in. She visits $3$ side shows and she has a cream tea.

How much does Mary spend altogether?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Mary goes to the school fair.

She pays to get in. She visits &#36;3&#36; side shows and she has a cream tea.

How much does Mary spend altogether?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.

</div>

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent} &= 20 \ \text{p} + 3 \times 30 \ \text{p} + \pounds 2.60 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 0.20 + 3 \times \pounds 0.30 + \pounds 2.60 \\\\
                    &= 0.20 + 0.90 + 2.60 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 3.70
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 3.70$

</div>
</div>

inherits parent tags: yes
</span>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Anna has $\pounds 5$ to spend at the school fair. She must pay to get in.

How many times can she visit a side show if she has a cream tea as well?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Anna has &#36;\pounds 5&#36; to spend at the school fair. She must pay to get in.

How many times can she visit a side show if she has a cream tea as well?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of entry and tea}   &= 20 \ \text{p} + \pounds 2.60 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 0.20 + \pounds 2.60 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 2.80 \\\\
\text {Money left}              &= 5 - 2.80 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 2.20 \\\\
\text {Number of side shows}    &= \dfrac {\pounds 2.20} {30 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {220 \ \text{p}} {30 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {220} {30} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {22 \cancel{0}} {3 \cancel{0}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {22} {3} \\\\
                                &= 7 \text{ remainder } 1 \\\\
                                &\approx 7
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$7$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of entry and tea}   &= 20 \ \text{p} + \pounds 2.60 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 0.20 + \pounds 2.60 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 2.80 \\\\
\text {Money left}              &= 5 - 2.80 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 2.20 \\\\
\text {Number of side shows}    &= \dfrac {\pounds 2.20} {30 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {220 \ \text{p}} {30 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {220} {30} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {22 \cancel{0}} {3 \cancel{0}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {22} {3} \\\\
                                &= 7 \text{ remainder } 1 \\\\
                                &\approx 7
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
