<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

At a party we ordered $4$ sausage rolls at $24 \ \text{pence}$ each, $5$ hotdogs at $81 \ \text{p}$ each and $7$
drinks at $20 \ \text{pence}$ each.

What is the total cost?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">At a party we ordered &#36;4&#36; sausage rolls at &#36;24 \ \text{pence}&#36; each, &#36;5&#36; hotdogs at &#36;81 \ \text{p}&#36; each and &#36;7&#36;
drinks at &#36;20 \ \text{pence}&#36; each.

What is the total cost?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= 4 \times 24  + 5 \times 81  + 7 \times 20 \\\\
                    &= 96 + 405 + 140 \\\\
                    &= 641 \ \text {pence} \\\\
                    &= \pounds 6.41
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 6.41$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out $50 \%$ of $58 \ \text{m}$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out &#36;50 \%&#36; of &#36;58 \ \text{m}&#36;.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 50 \% \ \text {of} \ 58 \ \text{m} \\\\
&= \dfrac {50} {100} \times 58 \\\\
&= \dfrac {50 \times 58} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {50 \times 2 \times 29} {50 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {50} \times \cancel {2} \times 29} {\cancel {50} \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 29 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

\end{aligned}
$

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
&= 10 \% \ \text {of} \ 130 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10} {100} \times 130 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times 130} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \cancel {0} \times 13 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
&= 13
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

**Question**

Divide $9380$ by $35$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Divide &#36;9380&#36; by &#36;35&#36;.
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 9380 \div 35 \\\\
&= \dfrac {9380} {35} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 7 \times 268} {5 \times 7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times \cancel {7} \times 268} {\cancel {5} \times \cancel {7}} \\\\
&= 268
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the greatest number of $28 \ \text{p}$ stamps which can be bought for $\pounds 5$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the greatest number of &#36;28 \ \text{p}&#36; stamps which can be bought for &#36;\pounds 5&#36;.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of stamps}    &= \dfrac {\pounds 5} {28 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {500 \ \text{p}} {28 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {4 \times 125} {4 \times 7} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {\cancel {4} \times 125} {\cancel {4} \times 7} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {125} {7} \\\\
                            &= 17 \ \text {remainder} \ 6 \\\\
                            &\approx 17
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$17$

</div>
</div>

                            &= \dfrac {500 \ \text{p}} {28 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {4 \times 125} {4 \times 7} \\\\

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I buy $3$ chocolate bars for $65\ \text{pence}$ each and $5$ bags of crisps for $55 \ \text{p}$ each. How much change do I get from a $\pounds5$ note?

- $30\ \text{p}$
- $35\ \text{p}$
- $40\ \text{p}$
- $45\ \text{p}$
- $50\ \text{p}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I buy &#36;3&#36; chocolate bars for &#36;65\ \text{pence}&#36; each and &#36;5&#36; bags of crisps for &#36;55 \ \text{p}&#36; each. How much change do I get from a &#36;\pounds5&#36; note?

- &#36;30\ \text{p}&#36;
- &#36;35\ \text{p}&#36;
- &#36;40\ \text{p}&#36;
- &#36;45\ \text{p}&#36;
- &#36;50\ \text{p}&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

\text {Money spent}     &= 3 \times 65 + 5 \times 55 \\\\
                        &= 195 + 275 \\\\
                        &= 470 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text {Change received} &= \pounds 5 - 470 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= 500 \ \text{p} - 470 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= 30 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Toy cars cost $84\ \text{p}$ each or $\pounds2.20$ for a box of three. What's the largest number of cars I can buy with $\pounds15?$

- $17$
- $18$
- $19$
- $20$
- $21$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Toy cars cost &#36;84\ \text{p}&#36; each or &#36;\pounds2.20&#36; for a box of three. What's the largest number of cars I can buy with &#36;\pounds15?&#36;

- &#36;17&#36;
- &#36;18&#36;
- &#36;19&#36;
- &#36;20&#36;
- &#36;21&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Lets find out how many boxes of $3$ toy cars can be bought, then we find how many individual toy cars can be bought with the remaining money. 

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of boxes}         &= \dfrac {15} {2.20} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {15 \times 10} {2.20 \times 10} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {150} {22} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {2 \times 75} {11 \times 2} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 75} {11 \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {75} {11} \\\\
                                &= 6 \ \text {remainder} \ 9 \\\\
                                &\approx 6 \\\\
\text {Money spent on boxes}   &= 6 \times \pounds 2.20 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 13.20 \\\\
\text {Money left}   &= \pounds 15 - \pounds 13.20 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 1.80 \\\\
                                &= 180 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text {Individual toys}         &= \dfrac {180 \ \text{p}} {84 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {12 \times 15} {7 \times 12} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {12} \times 15} {7 \times \cancel {12}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {15} {7} \\\\
                                &= 2 \ \text {remainder} \ 1 \\\\
                                &\approx 2 \\\\
\text {Total number of toys}    &= 3 \times 6 + 2 \\\\
                                &= 18 + 2 \\\\
                                &= 20
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>D</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_unit_no_dot
</code></pre>
</div>

&#36;
\begin{aligned}

                                &= \dfrac {15 \times 10} {2.20 \times 10} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {150} {22} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {2 \times 75} {11 \times 2} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 75} {11 \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {75} {11} \\\\
                                &= 6 \ \text {remainder} \ 9 \\\\
                                &\approx 6 \\\\
\text {Money spent on boxes}   &= 6 \times \pounds 2.20 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 13.20 \\\\
\text {Money left}   &= \pounds 15 - \pounds 13.20 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 1.80 \\\\
                                &= 180 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text {Individual toys}         &= \dfrac {180 \ \text{p}} {84 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {12 \times 15} {7 \times 12} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {12} \times 15} {7 \times \cancel {12}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {15} {7} \\\\
                                &= 2 \ \text {remainder} \ 1 \\\\
                                &\approx 2 \\\\

</div>
</div>

</div>

</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I buy $4$ Mega Bars at $65\ \text{pence}$ each and $3$ Star Bars at $83\ \text{pence}$ each. How much change do I get from $\pounds10?$ 

- $\pounds4.91$ 
- $\pounds5.09$ 
- $\pounds5.91$ 
- $\pounds5.19$ 
- $\pounds4.81$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I buy &#36;4&#36; Mega Bars at &#36;65\ \text{pence}&#36; each and &#36;3&#36; Star Bars at &#36;83\ \text{pence}&#36; each. How much change do I get from &#36;\pounds10?&#36; 

- &#36;\pounds4.91&#36; 
- &#36;\pounds5.09&#36; 
- &#36;\pounds5.91&#36; 
- &#36;\pounds5.19&#36; 
- &#36;\pounds4.81&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I buy $7$ bags of Cheezos at $55\ \text{pence}$ each and $4$ bags of Nuttees at $63\ \text{pence}$ each. How much change do I get from $\pounds{10}?$ 

- $\pounds{3.73}$ 
- $\pounds{3.63}$
- $\pounds{4.73}$ 
- $\pounds{4.63}$ 
- $\pounds{6.37}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I buy &#36;7&#36; bags of Cheezos at &#36;55\ \text{pence}&#36; each and &#36;4&#36; bags of Nuttees at &#36;63\ \text{pence}&#36; each. How much change do I get from &#36;\pounds{10}?&#36; 

- &#36;\pounds{3.73}&#36; 
- &#36;\pounds{3.63}&#36;
- &#36;\pounds{4.73}&#36; 
- &#36;\pounds{4.63}&#36; 
- &#36;\pounds{6.37}&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">

_no_dot
</div>

<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 7 \times 55 + 4 \times 63 \\\\
                        &= 385 + 252 \\\\
                        &= 637 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 6.37 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 10 - 6.37 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 3.63
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<p>B</p>

**Answer**

$\pounds 3.63$

</div>

</span>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many packs must be bought?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many packs must be bought?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Packs needed}            &= \dfrac {480} {25} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {5 \times 96} {5 \times 5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 96} {\cancel {5} \times 5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {96} {5} \\\\
                                &= 19 \ \text {remainder} \ 1 \\\\
                                &\approx 20
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20$

</div>
</div>

&= \pounds 8.58
\end{aligned}
$

$\dfrac{3} {4}$ of $180\degree$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

&= 3 \times 45 \\\\
&= 135 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

tmoney
</span>
</li>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Mrs Cook's supermarket basket contains: 

$1$ loaf of bread costing $68\ \text{p}$

$1$ packet of cheese costing $\pounds3.29$ 

$5$ packets of crisps at $22\ \text{p}$ each 

$2$ tins of soup at $39\ \text{p}$ each.

Calculate how much change she will receive if she pays with a $\pounds20$ note. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Mrs Cook's supermarket basket contains: 

&#36;1&#36; loaf of bread costing &#36;68\ \text{p}&#36;

&#36;1&#36; packet of cheese costing &#36;\pounds3.29&#36; 

&#36;5&#36; packets of crisps at &#36;22\ \text{p}&#36; each 

&#36;2&#36; tins of soup at &#36;39\ \text{p}&#36; each.

Calculate how much change she will receive if she pays with a &#36;\pounds20&#36; note. 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 68 \ \text{p} + \pounds 3.29 + 5 \times 22 \ \text{p} + 2 \times 39 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 0.68 + \pounds 3.29 + 5 \times \pounds 0.22 + 2 \times \pounds 0.39 \\\\
                        &= 0.68 + 3.29 + 1.1 + 0.78 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5.85 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 20 - 5.85 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 14.15
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 14.15$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$30\%$ of $\pounds 70$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;30\%&#36; of &#36;\pounds 70&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 30 \% \text { of } \pounds 70 \\\\
&= \dfrac {30} {100} \times 70 \\\\
&= \dfrac {30 \times 70} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \cancel {0} \times 7 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 7 \\\\
&= \pounds 21
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How much change should she get?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 4 \times 56 \\\\
                        &= 224 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 2.24 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 5 - 2.24 \\\\

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Mark bought $25$ melons for his stall. 
He paid $\pounds 16$ for $25$ melons.

Work out the price Mark paid for each melon, in pence.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Mark bought &#36;25&#36; melons for his stall. 
He paid &#36;\pounds 16&#36; for &#36;25&#36; melons.

Work out the price Mark paid for each melon, in pence.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of each melon}  &= \dfrac {\pounds 16} {25} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {1600 \ \text{p}} {25} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {25 \times 64} {25} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {\cancel {25} \times 64} {\cancel {25}} \\\\
                            &= 64 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out what profit Mark made in selling the melons, giving your answer as a fraction of the cost of buying the melons.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out what profit Mark made in selling the melons, giving your answer as a fraction of the cost of buying the melons.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">

<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Profit}              &= 24 - 16 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 8 \\\\
\text {Fraction}            &= \dfrac {8} {16} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {8} {8 \times 2} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {\cancel {8}} {\cancel {8} \times 2} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {1} {2}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Susan's bus fare is exactly $44 \ \text{p}$. If the driver can give her change, what is the smallest number of coins that can change hands when Susan pays to get on the bus? (Explain clearly)

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Susan's bus fare is exactly &#36;44 \ \text{p}&#36;. If the driver can give her change, what is the smallest number of coins that can change hands when Susan pays to get on the bus? (Explain clearly)
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- The coins available are: $1 \ \text{p}$, $2 \ \text{p}$, $5 \ \text{p}$, $10 \ \text{p}$, $20 \ \text{p}$, $50 \ \text{p}$, $\pounds 1$, $\pounds 2$.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

ABBR: We can find the answer using trial and error.



Let’s consider different ways Susan could pay, and how much change the driver gives back.

| Susan pays      | Change given    | Coins changing hands | Remarks                                                                                              |
| :-------------: | :-------------: | :-------------------:| :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| $44 \ \text{p}$ | $0$             | $4$                  | Exact payment: $20 \ \text{p} + 20 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p}$                         |
| $45 \ \text{p}$ | $1 \ \text{p}$  | $4$                  | $3$ coins given ($20 \ \text{p} + 20 \ \text{p} + 5 \ \text{p}$), $1$ coin returned ($1 \ \text{p}$) |
| $50 \ \text{p}$ | $6 \ \text{p}$  | $3$                  | $1$ coin given ($50 \ \text{p}$), $2$ coins returned ($5 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}$)                 |
| $\pounds 1$     | $56 \ \text{p}$ | $5$                  | $1$ coin given ($\pounds 1$), $3$ coins returned ($50 \ \text{p} + 5 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}$)              |

Smallest number of coins $= 3$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$3$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_coins_available
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_working_note_md_trial_and_error_find_answer


Let’s consider different ways Susan could pay, and how much change the driver gives back.

| Susan pays      | Change given    | Coins changing hands | Remarks                                                                                              |
| :-------------: | :-------------: | :-------------------:| :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| &#36;44 \ \text{p}&#36; | &#36;0&#36;             | &#36;4&#36;                  | Exact payment: &#36;20 \ \text{p} + 20 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p} + 2 \ \text{p}&#36;                         |
| &#36;45 \ \text{p}&#36; | &#36;1 \ \text{p}&#36;  | &#36;4&#36;                  | &#36;3&#36; coins given (&#36;20 \ \text{p} + 20 \ \text{p} + 5 \ \text{p}&#36;), &#36;1&#36; coin returned (&#36;1 \ \text{p}&#36;) |
| &#36;50 \ \text{p}&#36; | &#36;6 \ \text{p}&#36;  | &#36;3&#36;                  | &#36;1&#36; coin given (&#36;50 \ \text{p}&#36;), &#36;2&#36; coins returned (&#36;5 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}&#36;)                 |
| &#36;\pounds 1&#36;     | &#36;56 \ \text{p}&#36; | &#36;5&#36;                  | &#36;1&#36; coin given (&#36;\pounds 1&#36;), &#36;3&#36; coins returned (&#36;50 \ \text{p} + 5 \ \text{p} + 1 \ \text{p}&#36;)              |

Smallest number of coins &#36;= 3&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
